//= Collection of Rachel Quests 
//= Lost Child Quest:
//= - End of quest coincides with opening the Sanctuary.
//= - Variable in use: lost_boy (max 13)
//= Bruspetti Quest:
//= - [Official Conversion]
//= - Requires friendship quest as a prerequisite.
//= - Variable in use: rach_vice (max 24)
//= Ice Necklace Quest:
//= - iRO uses a different sprite for Urstia, and no cutins.
//= - Variable in use: ice_necklace_q (max 7)
//= Sanctuary Quest:
//= - Actually two quests in one. Donation and dungeon quest.
//= - Requires 10000 donations before players can start the quest.
//= - Variable in use: ra_tem_q (max 23) (unset at end)
//= - Variable in use: Once completed MISC_QUEST bit 8192 is set
//= - Variable in use: ra_have_donated (0 no/1 yes)
//= - Variable in use: $rachel_donate (max 10000)

ice_dun03,126,126,3	script	타오르는 불길#ice1::Blazing Fire#ice1	802,{
	mes needtr("^3355FF이 타오르는 불의 장벽은", "^3355FFThe flames in this fire");

	mes needtr("이 기세를 잠재울 수", "you can put this fire out");
	mes needtr("없을 것 같다.^000000", "with conventional means.^000000");
	if (ice_necklace_q > 4) {
		if ($@ktullanux_summon < 4) {
			if (countitem(7574) > 0) {
				next;
				select(needtr("빙설의 가루를 사용한다.", "Use the Frozen Powder."));
				mes needtr("^3355FF빙설의 가루를 화염에", "^3355FFYou sprinkle the Freezing Snow");
				mes needtr("뿌리자, 마력의 불길이 힘없이", "Powder onto the flame. It");
				mes needtr("사그라지며 소멸했다.^000000", "flickers before extinguising.^000000");
				delitem 7574,1; //Freezing Snow Powder
				hideonnpc "Blazing Fire#ice1";
				set $@ktullanux_summon,$@ktullanux_summon+1;
				if ($@ktullanux_summon == 4) donpcevent "ice_boss#broad::OnStart";
			}
			else if (countitem(7562) > 0) {
				next;
				select(needtr("얼음의 비늘을 사용한다.", "Use the Ice Scale."));
				mes needtr("^3355FF불길 속으로 얼음의 비늘을", "^3355FFYou throw the Ice Scale into");
				mes needtr("던지자, 격렬하게 타오르던", "the flame, it crackles before");
				mes needtr("불꽃이 이내 꺼졌다.^000000", "extinguising.^000000");
				delitem 7562,1; //Ice Scale
				hideonnpc "Blazing Fire#ice1";
				set $@ktullanux_summon,$@ktullanux_summon+1;
				if ($@ktullanux_summon == 4) donpcevent "ice_boss#broad::OnStart";
				close;
			}
		}
	}
	close;
}

OnStop:
	stopnpctimer;
	end;

OnStart:
	initnpctimer;
	end;

OnTimer2000:
	mapannouncelang "ice_dun03","감히 누가 토르의 불길을 꺼뜨린 것이냐... 불경한 놈!", "Someone has put out Thor's flames... Infidel!",bc_map,0xFF6633;
	end;

OnTimer8000:
	mapannouncelang "ice_dun03","이 크툴라눅스가 토르의 성스러운 불꽃을 수호할지어다...", "I, Ktullanux, must protect and preserve Thor's fierce flames...",bc_map,0xFF6633;
	end;

	end;
}

S_Donate:
	.@num = getarg(0);
	cutin "ra_nemma01",2;
	mes needtr("[사제 넴마]", "[Priestess Nemma]");
	mes needtr("그러니까, ", "So, ") + strcharinfo(0) + "...";
	mes needtr("확인차 다시 여쭤볼게요.:", "I just want to make sure:");
	mes needtr("제니를 기부하고 ", "You want to donate ")+getarg(1);
	mes needtr("그 대가로 ", "zeny, and receive ")+ .@num +needtr(" 추첨권을", " Lottery");
	if (.@num == 1) mes needtr("받으시려는 거죠? 맞나요?", "Ticket. Is that correct?");
	else mes needtr("받으시려는 거죠? 맞나요?", "Tickets. Is that correct?");
	next;
	if (select(needtr("아니요:네", "No:Yes")) == 1) {
		mes needtr("[사제 넴마]", "[Priestess Nemma]");
		mes needtr("아, 그렇군요. 제니가 부족하다면", "Oh, I see. Well, if you");
		mes needtr("나중에 여유가 생겼을 때", "don't have enough zeny with");
		mes needtr("다시 들러주셔도 돼요.", "you, then you can just come");
		mes needtr("프레이야 여신님은 관대한 이에게", "back and donate later. Our");
		mes needtr("미소를 지어주시고 인내하는 이에게", "goddess Freya smiles on the");
		mes needtr("복을 내려주신답니다!", "generous and rewards the patient!");
		return;
	}
	if (Zeny >= .@num*50000) {
		if (!checkweight(7570,.@num)) {
			cutin "ra_nemma02",2;
			mes needtr("[사제 넴마]", "[Priestess Nemma]");
			mes needtr("세상에, 믿기지 않네요...", "I can scarcely believe it...");
			mes needtr("짐을 어찌나 많이 들고 계신지", "You're carrying so much stuff,");

			mes needtr("창고에 짐을 좀 맡겨두고", "You'd better put some of");
			mes needtr("오시는 게 좋겠는데요?", "your things in Storage, yes?");
			next;
			cutin "ra_nemma01",2;

			mes needtr("오실 때까지 전 계속", "here after you make more");
			mes needtr("여기 있을 테니까요.", "space available in your");
			mes needtr("얼른 정리하고 오셔서", "Inventory. Hurry back, and");
			mes needtr("기부해 주실 거죠?", "donate if you can, okay?");
			return;
		}
		$rachel_donate = $rachel_donate + .@num;
		getitem 7570,.@num;// Temple_Lottery_Ticket
		Zeny = Zeny - .@num *50000;
		set ra_have_donated,1;
		if ($rachel_donate > 9999) {
			cutin "ra_nemma03",2;
			mes needtr("[사제 넴마]", "[Priestess Nemma]");
			mes needtr("여기 있었네요~", "There you are~");
			mes needtr("기부해 주셔서 정말 고마워요!", "Thanks so much for");
			mes needtr("프레이야 여신님께서 분명", "your donation! I'm sure");
			mes needtr("당신을 기특하게 여기시고", "that Freya is smiling down");
			mes needtr("당신의 너그러움에 보답을", "upon you, and will reward");
			mes needtr("내려주실 거예요~", "you for your generosity~");
			return;
		}
		else {
			set .@Remaining,10000-$rachel_donate;
			cutin "ra_nemma03",2;
			mes needtr("[사제 넴마]", "[Priestess Nemma]");
			mes needtr("정말 감사합니다! 목표 금액을", "Thank you so much! We'll be");

			mes needtr("50,000 제니 단위로 ", "In units of 50,000 Zeny, ")+.@Remaining+needtr(" 조금만 더 모이면", " a little more and");
			mes needtr("모금 활동은 이제", "in increments of 50,000 zeny,");
			mes needtr("마무리될 거예요.", "our fundraiser will finish.");
			next;

			mes needtr("혹시 마음이 내키신다면,", "If you're feeling so");
			mes needtr("나중에 다시 들러서", "inclined, come back later");
			mes needtr("기부를 부탁드릴게요.", "and make another contribution.");
			mes needtr("다시 한번 감사드려요. 프레이야", "Thanks again, and may Freya");
			mes needtr("여신님의 보호가 늘 함께하기를.", "always watch over you.");
			return;
		}
	}
	else {
		mes needtr("[사제 넴마]", "[Priestess Nemma]");
		mes needtr("죄송하지만, 가지고 계신 제니가", "I'm sorry, but you have");
		mes needtr("기부 최소 금액보다 ", "less than ")+ (.@num *50000) +needtr(" 적은 것 같네요...", " zeny...");
		mes needtr("큰돈을 부탁드리는 건 알지만,", "I know it's asking a lot,");
		mes needtr("정해진 규칙이라 어쩔 수 없어요.", "but those are the rules that");

		mes needtr("음, 다음에 여유가 생기면 오시겠어요?", "Well, maybe another time, yes?");
		return;
	}
}

	}
